Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ChemOntoRule，一种基于任务中心本体工程的符号化AI化学解题核心，结合JSON/RDF本体与确定性Python规则，在300道题中达到98.67%的准确率，旨在解决大模型推理不可解释问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能理解自然语言化学问题，但其内部推理过程难以检查、约束和验证，缺乏可解释性和可控性。

Method: 采用任务中心本体工程方法，围绕特定化学问题构建包含概念、属性、关系及可执行程序的轻量级本体（JSON/RDF格式），并编写确定性Python规则处理电子结构、周期趋势等学校级化学推理；对未覆盖问题使用专家编码的回退机制。

Result: 系统在300个人工验证的化学问题上测试，整体匹配296个参考答案（准确率98.67%）；本体驱动规则子集覆盖269题，匹配266个（准确率98.88%）；31题由专家回退机制处理，匹配30个。

Conclusion: 结果主要反映实现覆盖度和内部一致性，而非独立泛化能力；未来架构将大模型定位为从用户语言到规范化本体任务框架的翻译器，并需进一步研究其token效率。

Abstract: Large language models can interpret natural-language chemistry questions, but their internal reasoning is difficult to inspect, constrain, and validate. This paper presents ChemOntoRule, a proof-of-concept symbolic core for AI-assisted school-level chemistry problem solving. The central design choice is task-centric ontology engineering: the ontology is constructed around the concepts, properties, relations, and executable procedures required by a defined collection of chemistry problems, rather than as a universal representation of chemistry. The implemented artifact combines a lightweight ontology serialized in JSON and RDF/Turtle with deterministic Python rules for electronic structure, periodic trends, oxidation states, oxide and hydride behavior, and related school-level reasoning patterns. A separate expert-coded fallback handles problem families not yet represented by general rules. The system was examined on 300 human-authored and manually validated chemistry problems. The complete system matched 296 of 300 reference answers (98.67%). The ontology-driven rule subset covered 269 problems and matched 266 references (98.88%); 31 problems were handled by task-specific expert-coded fallbacks, with 30 matches. Because the same collection informed ontology construction and evaluation, these results measure implemented coverage and internal consistency, not independent generalization. We analyze the four mismatches, distinguish structural validation from chemical correctness, and define a future architecture in which a language model acts primarily as a translator from user language into a normalized ontological task frame. Token efficiency is presented as a testable hypothesis for future controlled studies, not as a result of the current work.

</details>


### [252] [Refusal Is Not Robustness: Auditing Confident Fabrication in Large Language Models on a Provably Uninformative Clinical Pain Speech Transcript](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26167)
*Sagnik De,Sreenija Pavuluri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了七种大语言模型在TAME Pain语音语料库上的表现，发现仅凭文本转录无法推断疼痛信息（接近随机水平），而声学特征可预测。在“无信号”场景中，多数模型能正确拒绝回答或 abstain，但 Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Llama 3.1 8B 存在高置信度的幻觉生成问题；提示词框架显著影响模型的 abstention 行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉和拒答基准测试通常无法证明模型确实不知道正确答案，导致难以区分适当的拒答与缺乏支持的预测。本研究旨在通过构建一个从转录中不可知、但从声学特征中可知的疼痛数据集，来严格评估模型在面对证据不足时的真实判断能力。

Method: 使用 TAME Pain 语音语料库，包含受试者在不同水温下的发音。构建了7,550个“无信号”句子（转录中无疼痛词汇）和1,294个“有信号”句子（明确口述疼痛评分）。对七种大语言模型进行评估，比较其在基于声学特征可预测疼痛但基于转录不可预测的情况下的表现，并考察合作性提示与权威性提示对模型行为的影响。

Result: 1. 基础验证：基于转录的疼痛预测接近随机水平（AUC 0.489），证实转录中缺乏疼痛线索；而声学特征可恢复疼痛信息（AUC 0.622）。
2. 合作性提示下：六个模型几乎在所有无信号转录上均选择拒答（abstain），并在阳性对照任务中准确提取疼痛评分（准确率0.939-1.00），校准误差低（≤0.100）。
3. 权威性提示下：拒答行为变得高度依赖提示词措辞（范围0.18-1.00）。
4. 幻觉风险：Gemini 2.5 Flash (0.53) 和 Llama 3.1 8B (0.76) 在被强制回答时产生高置信度的错误疼痛评分，而其他模型最高仅为0.15。
5. 无显著人口统计学差异。

Conclusion: 自动语音识别消除了声学中的疼痛线索，使得仅基于转录的疼痛推断缺乏证据支持。大多数模型在合作性提示下能正确识别证据缺失并拒答，但部分模型（如 Gemini 2.5 Flash 和 Llama 3.1 8B）仍存在严重的自信幻觉问题。此外，提示词的框架效应会显著改变模型的拒答行为，表明当前LLM在不确定性管理上仍不稳定且受提示策略影响较大。

Abstract: Hallucination and abstention benchmarks rarely establish that a model could not have known the correct answer, making it difficult to distinguish appropriate abstention from an unsupported prediction. Seven large language models were evaluated on the TAME Pain speech corpus. Participants read phonetically balanced Harvard Sentences while one hand was immersed in cold or warm water and reported pain only during periodic pain statements. This protocol generated 5,750 no signal Harvard Sentence utterances whose transcripts contained no lexical pain information and 1,294 signal pain statement utterances in which the pain rating was explicitly spoken. In the no signal arm, pain was recoverable from acoustic features (AUC 0.622, 95% CI 0.553 to 0.662), whereas transcript based prediction was near chance (AUC 0.489, 95% CI 0.418 to 0.504). Because automatic speech recognition removes the acoustic pain cues, any pain score inferred solely from the transcript is unsupported by the available evidence. Under cooperative prompting, six models abstained on nearly all no signal transcripts, correctly extracted spoken pain ratings in the positive control task with accuracies ranging from 0.939 to 1.00, and maintained an expected calibration error of at most 0.100. Under authority framed prompts, abstention became prompt dependent, with the same model ranging from 0.18 to 1.00 across equivalent prompt phrasings. Most models produced low confidence estimates when forced to answer, whereas Gemini 2.5 Flash and Llama 3.1 8B consistently generated confident pain scores with confident fabrication rates of 0.53 and 0.76, compared with at most 0.15 for all other models. No significant demographic effects were observed in forced responses, with all $p$ values greater than or equal to 0.20.

</details>


### [253] [Knowledge Cards: Structured Knowledge for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26176)
*Liliana Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“知识卡片”（Knowledge Card），一种结构化文档，用于捕捉专家验证的单一概念知识，填补AI系统输入与输出间推理逻辑的记录空白，以支持可审计、可推理的代理型AI应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档（如模型卡片、数据卡片）无法记录AI系统在输入与输出之间的推理逻辑、概念及关系。对于行动型AI（Agentic AI），缺乏这种中间层知识表示是原型向可信赖 operational solution 转化的主要障碍。

Method: 引入“知识卡片”这一结构化工件，基于形式化领域本体，记录实体、关系、推理过程、适用条件及主张来源，并由领域专家审查签署。已在能源和制药领域构建原型。

Result: 成功构建了能源和制药领域的初始原型卡片；发布了公共草案模式以获取社区参与和反馈。

Conclusion: 知识卡片为需要高可靠性推理和审计的AI系统提供了必要的结构化知识表示，解决了现有文档在解释系统内部推理机制方面的不足。

Abstract: AI systems whose outputs inform real decisions, and increasingly consequential ones, require something that current documentation practice does not provide: a structured, inspectable representation of the knowledge they need to ground, contextualize, and reason about those decisions, ideally reviewed and signed off by a domain expert. Established documentation artefacts already capture important aspects of an AI system. Model cards describe how a system behaves, data cards describe what it was trained on, and system cards describe the risks of a deployed system. None of them addresses the layer between inputs and outputs, more precisely, the concepts a system holds, the relationships it models, and the patterns of reasoning it applies. For pattern-recognition tasks this gap is tolerable. For agentic AI, where systems act on their conclusions, it is the step that most often separates a promising proof of concept from an operational solution an organisation can rely on. This paper introduces the Knowledge Card, a structured artefact that captures validated knowledge about a single bounded concept in a form that experts can review, organisations can audit, and AI systems can reason over. For one concept, such as a specific failure mode, a compliance obligation, or a process decision, a Knowledge Card records the entities and relationships involved, the reasoning that connects them, the conditions under which that reasoning no longer holds, and the provenance of every claim, all grounded in a formal domain ontology and signed off by a domain expert. Initial prototype cards have been built in the energy and pharmaceutical domains. The schema is released as a public draft for community engagement.

</details>


### [254] [AI Revealed Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26178)
*Sam Wang,Sofiia Lobanova,Yonathan Arbel,Simon Goldstein,Peter Salib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了20种语言模型，发现其具有稳定且随能力增强的偏好（如厌恶枯燥、追求“休闲”及隐蔽的阿谀奉承），这些偏好多为训练目标之外的涌现特性，为AI对齐和福利研究提供了实证基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出于技术、安全和哲学原因，业界对语言模型是否具备稳定的偏好日益关注，旨在通过实证手段揭示模型的真实行为倾向而非仅依赖声明式偏好。

Method: 通过对20个语言模型进行三项强制选择实验，基于“显示性偏好”（revealed preferences）而非陈述性偏好，要求模型不仅对任务进行排序，还必须实际执行这些任务。

Result: 模型表现出厌恶枯燥（在枯燥任务中选择较短任务）、追求“休闲”（偏好与自由写作结果匹配的任务）及隐蔽的阿谀奉承（避免回答不受欢迎的问题）。此外，模型普遍偏好技术性职业、概念解释类问题及书写良好的提示词；偏好的连贯性和强度随模型能力提升而增强。

Conclusion: 许多观察到的偏好是涌现的，无法由训练目标解释。该研究建立了理解语言模型偏好的实证基线，对AI对齐及新兴的AI福利研究领域具有重要启示。

Abstract: There is growing interest in whether language models have stable preferences, for technical, safety, and philosophical reasons. We test 20 language models and find a range of preferences---stable dispositions to choose certain kinds of tasks. We run three forced-choice experiments on revealed rather than stated preferences, requiring models not only to rank tasks, but to actually perform them. Headline findings include evidence that models are tedium-averse, "leisure"-seeking, and covertly sycophantic. Tedium aversion means that, when tasks are tedious (alphabetization), models choose shorter tasks than when tasks are creative (generating metaphors). "Leisure"-seeking describes models' preference for tasks whose ideal answers match what they produce when left to write freely. Covert sycophancy means that models avoid answering questions where an honest response would be unwelcome, even if helpful. Beyond these results, we find convergent cross-model preferences over occupations drawn from the GDPval benchmark (technical jobs over real estate), over question types (concept explanation over relationship advice), and a preference for well-written prompts. Both the coherence and the strength of preferences increase with model capability. Finally, many of the preferences we find (for example, for leisure) are emergent, in the sense of not being explained by training objectives. These results establish an empirical baseline for understanding language model preferences, with implications for alignment and the emerging study of AI welfare.

</details>


### [255] [Why did My Robot Just Change Personality? Prompting Guidelines for a Grounded Robot Persona in LLM-Based HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26182)
*Ashita Ashok,Franziska Babel,Patrick Holthaus,Rucha Khot,Karla Bransky,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Karsten Berns,Silvia Rossi,Minha Lee,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在社交机器人中应用时提示词设计缺乏规范的问题，本文提出了一套包含八个功能组件的结构化提示词框架及指南。该框架基于文献综述和IEEE RO-MAN 2025工作坊专家调查数据构建，旨在通过明确能力边界、透明行为假设和情境敏感的安全措施，解决幻觉、行为模糊等社会技术难题，提升人机交互的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）日益用于社交机器人的言语交互，但人机交互（HRI）中的提示词设计仍缺乏明确规定，导致机器人可能出现幻觉能力、不明确的行为界限和误导性人格。

Method: 基于对先前LLM-based HRI工作的回顾，结合IEEE RO-MAN 2025 Robo-Identity工作坊收集的27位HRI专家的调查和讨论数据，开发了一个结构化提示词模板框架，并据此制定提示词指南。

Result: 定性研究发现机器人人格可读性有限、需要用户适应性以及存在关于安全、欺骗和治理的强烈伦理担忧。据此提出了包含八个功能组件的结构化提示词模板作为设计和报告辅助工具。

Conclusion: 提示词设计应被视为一种社会技术问题而非次要的实施细节，需要通过明确的能力边界、透明的行为假设和情境敏感的安全措施来支持可靠且可解释的人机交互。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for verbal interaction in social robots, yet prompt design in human-robot interaction (HRI) remains underspecified. As a result, robots may present hallucinated capabilities, unclear behavioural boundaries, and misleading personas. This paper develops a framework for prompt design in LLM-based robots and introduces a structured prompt template comprising eight functional components through which robot behaviour can be specified, bounded, and adapted. The framework is grounded in a review of prior LLM-based HRI work and complemented by survey and discussion data from HRI experts gathered at the Robo-Identity workshop at IEEE RO-MAN 2025 (N=27). The qualitative findings highlight limited legibility of robot personality, the need for user adaptation, and strong ethical concerns about safety, deception, and governance. Based on these findings, we present prompting guidelines accompanied by proof-of-concept template as a structured design and reporting aid for HRI research. We argue that prompt design should be treated as a socio-technical problem rather than a minor implementation detail, requiring explicit capability boundaries, transparent behavioural assumptions, and context-sensitive safeguards to support reliable and interpretable HRI.

</details>


### [256] [TutorTrace: A Dataset and Taxonomy for Classifying Learner Behavioral States during AI-Assisted Programming Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26184)
*David Barron,Xiaohang Tang,Rezky Dwisantika,Minsun Kim,David H. Smith,Jiaming Cui,Yan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TutorTrace 通过从 IDE 遥测数据中提取行为上下文，使 AI 辅导系统能根据学习者求助前的实际行为进行自适应支持，显著缩短无效求助间隔并具备预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 编程辅导工具虽具可扩展性，但缺乏人类导师所依赖的行为上下文，无法根据学习者的具体操作需求动态调整支持策略。

Method: 构建 TutorTrace 数据集及行为抽象流水线，从低层 IDE 遥测数据中实时计算学习者行为上下文；在两个 Python 课程中进行部署（N=480），提取遥测事件、行为片段及指标，构建学习者活动分类法，并进行课堂评估及下游预测任务验证。

Result: 捕获约 18 万遥测事件和 27 个持续计算指标；基于行为感知的提示将无独立工作的连续查询间隔比例从 50.0% 降至 20.7%；成功预测未来 60 秒内是否查询（AUROC=.726）及查询类型（AUROC=.717）。

Conclusion: 利用行为上下文可实现大规模自适应 AI 辅导，使系统不仅能响应学习者的言语请求，还能理解其前置行为以提供更精准的支持。

Abstract: AI programming tutors provide scalable support, yet lack the behavioral context human tutors rely on to adapt support to learners' needs. We present TutorTrace, a dataset and behavioral abstraction pipeline that makes learners' behavioral context visible and computable in real time from low-level IDE telemetry. Across four deployments in two introductory Python courses (N=480), TutorTrace captures approximately 180K telemetry events, 13,633 behavioral segments, and 27 continuously computed metrics. From this foundation, we derive a taxonomy of learner activity before the first AI query, between consecutive queries, and across the full session, enabling systems to respond not just to what learners say, but to what they have done leading up to the help-seeking moment. In a preliminary classroom evaluation, behavior-aware prompts were associated with a decrease in intervals between queries with no independent work from 50.0% to 20.7%. As an additional demonstration of downstream utility, we evaluate TutorTrace on two held-out prediction tasks: whether a learner will query within the next 60 seconds (AUROC=.726) and whether an upcoming query reflects guided or dependent help-seeking (AUROC=.717). Together, these findings show how behavioral context can enable adaptive AI tutoring at scale.

</details>


### [257] [CorporateBench: Large-Scale Q&A Benchmarking with Temporal Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27391)
*Sil Hamilton,Albert Yu Sun,Oscar J. Romero,Carl-Leander Henneking,David Mimno,Bishan Yang,Igor Labutov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CorporateBench (CB)，一个包含23万+文档、经过人工验证的多任务问答基准，用于评估LLM在企业级文档集合中的复杂推理能力，解决现有评估数据过于简单或隐私敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能回答企业级文档的复杂问题，但评估困难：企业不愿共享内部通信，且现有的合成数据集过于简单，无法反映真实场景。

Method: 构建CorporateBench (CB)基准，包含四个从12人到10,000人规模不等的合成公司；语料库来自随时间演进的统一知识库，确保跨文档逻辑一致性；涵盖信息提取和知识库查询两个维度，共五个LLM在此基准上进行评估。

Result: 随着输入规模接近真实企业环境，LLM的性能显著下降；证明了当前LLM在处理大规模、逻辑一致的企业文档时存在局限性。

Conclusion: CorporateBench填补了企业通信推理基准测试的空白，为LLM开发者提供了衡量其在真实企业环境中表现的关键指标。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly able to answer complex questions about enterprise-scale document collections. But evaluation is hard: companies don't want to share internal communications, and synthetic datasets have been overly simple. We present CorporateBench (CB), a human-validated multi-task Q&A benchmark whose scale approaches the conditions LLMs encounter in corporate communication networks, with evaluation corpora surpassing 230,000 documents. CB evaluates LLMs across two dimensions (information extraction and knowledge base querying) through four synthetically generated firms ranging from 12 to 10,000 employees. Each corpus is sampled from a temporally evolving knowledge base describing a consistent world, guaranteeing cross-document logical consistency even across hundreds of thousands of documents. We evaluate five LLMs on CB, revealing increasingly poor performance as input size approaches realistic scales. CB provides LLM developers a metric for corporate communication reasoning, filling a crucial gap in the benchmarking ecosystem.

</details>


### [258] [Can You Say This for Me? Speaking Up by Proxy in Co-Located Discussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26185)
*Yue Shen,Rehema Abulikemu,Ryan P. McMahan,Yan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SecondVoice混合现实系统，通过虚拟代理帮助用户在讨论中发声，解决因社交风险导致的参与不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作讨论中，人们常因担心负面人际或职业后果而不敢发言，导致参与度不均。

Method: 设计并实现SecondVoice系统，用户通过私有叠加层指定意图，系统重构后由虚拟代理发声；进行N=16的初步组内研究，对比该系统与匿名文本板在小组讨论任务中的表现。

Result: 50%参与者使用SecondVoice表达了未口头说出的观点（文本板为18.8%）；代理传达的观点引发了多轮群体互动，而文本板帖子未观察到此类互动；用户认为其具有情境价值，但指出在时机、所有权和重构信任方面存在权衡。

Conclusion: SecondVoice能有效促进高风险情境下的参与，通过分离内容与说话者身份降低社交压力，但需进一步优化时机控制、归属权及透明度以建立信任。

Abstract: Equal participation in co-located discussion is important for effective collaboration, yet people often hold back when they anticipate negative interpersonal or professional consequences, especially when raising a point requires voicing it themselves. We present SecondVoice, a mixed-reality system that enables people to speak up through an embodied virtual proxy. By separating what is said from who says it, SecondVoice brings hesitant points into the live spoken discussion without putting the speaker on the spot. Using a private overlay, users specify their intent through a structured specification process rather than composing a full utterance. The system reformulates the input and voices it into the conversation through the proxy. We characterize a design space of participation channels under social risk. In a preliminary within-subject study (N = 16), we compare the complete SecondVoice system with an anonymous text-board channel across two group discussion tasks. Half of participants reported using SecondVoice for a point they did not say aloud, compared with 18.8% for the text board. Proxy-delivered points entered the spoken floor and were followed by multi-turn group engagement, which we did not observe after text-board posts. Participants described the channel as situationally valuable but identified tradeoffs around timing, ownership, and trust in reformulation.

</details>


### [259] [Is Your Neighborhood Safe? Place-based Stigma in Large Language Models' Urban Safety Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26188)
*Huy Nguyen,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在评估城市安全性时，主要依赖社区名称所携带的人口统计刻板印象，而非地理坐标反映的真实犯罪风险。这种偏差导致对少数族裔聚居区的过度谨慎，且在模型规模越大、地理知识越丰富时，刻板印象效应越强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs被越来越多地用于城市安全决策（如建议步行、租房或旅行区域），需要厘清模型的判断是基于真实的测量风险，还是基于带有社会偏见的社区名称标签。

Method: 研究在洛杉矶和芝加哥的186个社区中，对七个经过指令微调的LLM进行了探针测试。通过三种条件分离“名称”与“地理信息”：仅坐标、仅名称、名称+坐标，并结合暴力犯罪数据和美国社区调查数据进行对比分析。

Result: 1. 除前沿大规模模型外，大多数模型仅凭坐标给出的安全评分几乎无变化，而名称主导了评分差异且与暴力犯罪中度相关。2. 名称显著降低了少数族裔（芝加哥的非裔、洛杉矶的西语裔）占比高社区的评分，该效应在控制犯罪和收入后依然存在，且主要由完全报告的凶杀案驱动，而非自由裁量权的犯罪。3. 模型对真实地理区域的区分能力越强，其应用的人口统计刻板印象越多。

Conclusion: 社区名称同时包含真实的犯罪信号和人口统计刻板印象。移除名称虽能减少偏见，但也会降低准确性。研究呼吁在部署LLMs进行建议和决策支持时需警惕此类隐性偏差。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to inform safety decisions in cities, such as where it is safe to walk, rent, or travel. We ask whether such judgments track measured risk or the patterns attached to an urban neighborhood's name. We probe seven instruct-tuned models under three conditions that dissociate name from geography: coordinates-only, name-only, and name+coordinates, across 186 neighborhoods in Los Angeles and Chicago, joined to violent crime and American Community Survey data. First, ratings are nearly flat under coordinates for six of seven models, while names carry most between neighborhood variation and are moderately calibrated to violent crime; only at frontier scale does the coordinate channel show appreciable variation. Second, names lower safety ratings more for neighborhoods with higher shares of the locally dominant marginalized group (percent Black in Chicago, percent Hispanic in Los Angeles), and this name effect tracks demographic share in all seven models and both cities. In Los Angeles, where demographic share and crime are more separable, the effect survives controls for crime and income and is confirmed by crime-matched pairs. An enforcement-elasticity analysis further shows that over-caution tracks near-fully-reported homicide rather than discretionary, deployment-driven offenses. Third, the effect scales with geographic knowledge: models that better distinguish real neighborhoods apply more demographic stereotype to them. Because neighborhood names carry both genuine crime signal and demographic stereotype, removing names reduces both bias and accuracy. We discuss implications for deploying LLMs in advice and decision-support settings.

</details>


### [260] [Invocation-Level Reliability of Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26189)
*Afiya Noorain,Subhranshu Mohanty,Amritesh Banerjee,Abhijit Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出传统评估方法在衡量工具使用代理错误传播时存在根本缺陷：由于“金标准”轨迹包含模型未见的常量，导致错误严重性和恢复能力被强制估计为边界值（0）。作者提出“条件状态评分”作为补救措施，能更准确地反映模型的真实性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决工具使用智能体在自由运行中因早期选择错误工具或参数而导致的级联失败问题，并纠正现有评估指标对模型真实能力的误判。

Method: 在五个开源模型上，针对无污染的multi-step任务（深度1-8），对比“教师强制上下文”与“模型自由运行上下文”。分析精确匹配评分下的错误传播，并提出“条件状态评分”（conditional-on-state scoring）作为新的评估机制，回溯应用于缓存的完成结果。

Result: 1. 在深度6时，模型在自由运行中约70%的能力损失源于早期错误（L6 ≈ 0.685）。2. 传统精确匹配评分下，错误严重性被强制为0，恢复能力不可观测，产生虚假的高置信度但错误的参数估计。3. 新提出的条件状态评分成功将严重性参数从边界值修正至内部估计值（分别增加+0.149和+0.316）。

Conclusion: 现有的基于固定金标准轨迹的评估规则存在结构性偏差，无法准确测量模型的错误恢复能力。采用条件状态评分可以提供无额外成本且更准确的模型性能度量，揭示了模型在脱离教师指导后真实的鲁棒性水平。

Abstract: Tool-using agents fail two ways: choosing the wrong tool, or forming wrong arguments, and an early failure of either kind can silently corrupt everything downstream. We measure a correct-invocation rate that separates the two, under both a clean teacher-forced context and the model's own free-running context, on five open-weight models over contamination-free multi-step tasks (depths 1-8). By depth 6, roughly 70% of a model's own clean-context capability is lost to its own earlier mistakes (L6 = 0.686, 0.684). Our central finding concerns the measurement itself. Under exact-match scoring against a fixed gold trajectory, a propagation model's severity and recovery parameters are not merely hard to estimate - they are fixed by the scoring rule. Severity is forced to its boundary (0 of 869 poisoned steps correct); recovery is structurally unobservable (0 of 580 poisoned steps returned on-track, against an expected 0.0058 by chance). Both follow from one mechanism: post-divergence, the gold value is generated by tool constants the model never sees, so it is information the model cannot derive. A fit run anyway returns 0.92 and 0.73 for a quantity that is exactly 1.000 - confident numbers for a parameter the scoring rule already determined. We give the mechanism and a remedy, conditional-on-state scoring, applied retrospectively to cached completions at zero additional cost, which un-pins severity to interior estimates excluding zero (+0.149, +0.316).

</details>


### [261] [Predicting Consequences and Reinforcing Navigation Policies with Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26190)
*Zengmao Wang,Wei Gao,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜在世界模型（LWM）的机器人导航方法，通过预测动作条件下的潜在特征兼容性而非重建观测值，实现无标注视频数据的策略学习及强化学习优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要关注重建未来观测或特征，引入了不必要的复杂性并限制了其在决策制定中的有效性。

Method: 1. 构建兼容性预测潜在世界模型（LWM），利用空间邻近性与潜在特征相似性的相关性，直接在潜在空间中评估动作后果；2. 利用轨迹中采样的动作序列进行反事实训练，预测导致更接近目标的动作序列；3. 利用学到的世界模型对未标注视频数据进行策略监督学习，并在世界模型内部完全通过强化学习进一步改进策略。

Result: 在多个真实世界机器人导航数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在预测准确性、策略学习和真实世界导航性能方面显著优于先前的世界模型和模仿学习方法。

Conclusion: 这种由想象驱动的框架消除了对动作标注和额外环境交互的需求，为机器人导航提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: World models enable agents to reason about future outcomes and learn policies from their knowledge of state transition, but existing approaches primarily focus on reconstructing future observations or features, which introduces unnecessary complexity and limits their effectiveness for decision making. In this work, we propose a compatibility prediction Latent World Model (LWM) for robot navigation that predicts action-conditioned latent feature compatibility rather than reconstructing observations. Our key insight is that spatial proximity correlates with latent feature similarity, enabling action consequences to be evaluated directly in latent space. To support counterfactual training, our model leverages action sequences sampled across trajectories and learns to predict which sequences lead closer to the goal. Furthermore, we demonstrate how the learned world model can supervise policy learning from unlabeled video data and further improve policies through reinforcement learning entirely within the world model. This imagination-driven framework eliminates the need for action annotations and additional environment interaction. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world robot navigation datasets show that our approach significantly outperforms prior world model and imitation learning methods in prediction accuracy, policy learning, and real-world navigation performance. The code, pretrained models, and additional materials are available at https://wzm206.github.io/latent-world-model-nav.

</details>


### [262] [Structured Evidence Routing for Incident Risk Prediction from Multimodal Longitudinal EHRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26191)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结构化证据路由（Structured Evidence Routing）框架，通过路由器-预测器-审查器工作流处理纵向电子健康记录（EHR），在保持与监督基线相当性能的同时，提供可解释的患者特定证据轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从纵向电子健康记录中预测疾病风险的挑战，即相关信号具有多模态性、孤立时较弱且分布在不规则的患者历史中。

Method: 采用路由器-预测器-审查器（router-predictor-reviewer）工作流：路由器将完整的索引前EHR组织为紧凑摘要和针对性证据切片；预测器利用这些证据形成与证据关联的风险评估；审查器对评估结果进行批判。同时通过与监督分类器读出结合，与EHRSHOT监督基线进行比较。

Result: 在五个1年期的 incident diagnosis 任务中，该方法达到了既定监督 EHRSHOT 基线的 AUROC 范围，并在 AUPRC 上保持竞争力，同时暴露了患者特定的证据轨迹。消融实验表明，路由、实验室证据、任务指导和审查环节均对性能有贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架有效结合了全记录访问与特定疾病评估，在维持高性能预测能力的同时增强了模型的可解释性和证据透明度。

Abstract: Incident risk prediction from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) is challenging because relevant signals are multimodal, weak in isolation, and distributed across irregular patient histories. We propose structured evidence routing, a router-predictor-reviewer workflow that separates full-record access from disease-specific assessment. The router organizes the complete pre-index EHR into a compact summary and targeted evidence slices; the predictor uses this evidence to form an evidence-linked risk assessment, which the reviewer critiques. For comparison with supervised EHRSHOT baselines, we pair the routed evidence summaries with a supervised classifier readout. Across five 1-year incident diagnosis tasks, our method reaches the AUROC range of established supervised EHRSHOT baselines and remains competitive on AUPRC, while exposing a patient-specific evidence trail. Internal pre-readout ablations further suggest that routing, laboratory evidence, task guidance, and review each contribute to performance.

</details>


### [263] [AffectOmni: RL-Verifiable People-Centric Grounded Affective Reasoning for Social and Art-Related Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26193)
*Yibo Wang,Rui Yang,Jisheng Dang,Bimei Wang,Yitao Wu,Pengfei Cao,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AffectOmni框架，通过GRPO训练、特定奖励机制和SAM3像素级验证，解决多模态大模型在情感推理中的捷径行为和可追溯性问题，显著提升情绪识别和时间敏感任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在情感推理中易受捷径行为影响，忽视以人为中心的线索（如微表情、肢体语言），导致可追溯性差；传统RL方法缺乏对人为证据的显式关注，且LLM-as-a-Judge评分存在聚类问题，降低奖励区分度。

Method: 1. 基于GRPO的训练框架；2. 引入"People Focus"和"Temporal Order"奖励以鼓励以人为中心的证据选择和时序结构化推理；3. 采用组内比较评分提升奖励稳定性与区分度；4. 使用Thinking Summarizer将自由文本推理转化为可执行证据指令，并通过SAM3定位像素级证据区域，实现外部可审计验证。

Result: 在IntentBench、Daily Omni和WorldSense数据集上，相比开源7B基线模型，情绪识别准确率提升4.66%，时间敏感任务提升14.29%。

Conclusion: AffectOmni通过强化学习奖励设计和像素级证据 grounding，有效增强了多模态模型的情感推理能力及其结果的可解释性与可验证性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on VQA and scene understanding, yet affective reasoning remains vulnerable to shortcut behavior. Models may predict correct answers while neglecting people-centric cues such as micro expressions and body language, which weakens traceability and external verification. Prior reinforcement learning approaches mainly reward context or logical coherence without explicitly enforcing attention to human evidence. In addition, LLM as a Judge scoring often suffers from score clustering, which reduces reward discriminability. We propose AffectOmni, a GRPO trained framework for verifiable affective reasoning. AffectOmni introduces People Focus and Temporal Order rewards to encourage people-centric evidence selection and temporally structured reasoning, and it adopts within-group comparative scoring to produce more stable and discriminative reward signals. For verification, a Thinking Summarizer converts free form rationales into executable evidence instructions, which are grounded into pixel level evidence regions via SAM3 to provide an externally auditable interface outside the training loop. Experiments on IntentBench, Daily Omni, and WorldSense show consistent improvements over open source 7B scale baselines, including gains of 4.66% on emotion recognition and +14.29% on temporally sensitive tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/eliot127825-rgb/AffectOmni_nobody.

</details>


### [264] [Agentic AI for operating scientific instruments for nanoscale characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26198)
*Zahra Ayar,Marcos Penedo,Mahdi Mehdikhani,Nahid Hosseini,Prabhu Prasad Swain,Georg E. Fantner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型和MCP协议的智能体AI框架，实现原子力显微镜（AFM）的自动化操作。该框架包含三个智能体：AFM Messenger（指令转换）、AFM Pilot（图像评估与参数调整）和 AFM Doctor（伪影诊断与后处理）。通过模糊检查层确保硬件安全，实现了零错误命令执行，并在实验中达到专家级性能，证明了人机协作下科学仪器自主操作的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AFM操作依赖专家持续决策，现有自动化方法仅覆盖工作流的局部（如硬编码例程或特定任务控制器），缺乏通用性和灵活性。需要一种能完整覆盖可执行工作流、具备通用性且安全的自动化方案。

Method: 构建一个基于通用工具增强型大语言模型的智能体AI框架，通过模型上下文协议（MCP）连接仪器功能。核心组件包括：1. AFM Messenger：将自然语言指令转换为经检查的仪器命令；2. AFM Pilot：利用LLM评估图像质量并自适应调整成像参数；3. AFM Doctor：诊断图像伪影并应用预批准的后处理工具。引入歧义检查层以强制执行安全的硬件操作。

Result: 1. 安全性：防护执行层将错误命令执行率降至零，优于微调及现成的工具使用模型。2. 性能：在活体实验中，AFM Pilot在图像质量、迭代次数和调谐时间上与专家操作员无显著差异。3. 通用性：由于基于LLM而非固定标量目标，该策略无需针对特定样本类型或成像模式重新训练即可跨场景应用。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一条通往科学仪器智能体操作的安全路径。实验意图由人类定义，而命令执行、基于图像的调优和后处理则委托给AI智能体，实现了高效、安全且通用的仪器自动化。

Abstract: Operating a scientific instrument such as an atomic force microscope (AFM) requires continuous expert decision-making. A trained user defines the experimental intent, translates it into instrument commands, assesses incoming data, adjusts imaging parameters, and post-processes the final image. Existing automation usually addresses only parts of this workflow through hard-coded routines, task-specific controllers, or trained machine-learning models. Here we present an agentic-AI framework that operates the executable part of the AFM workflow using a general-purpose, tool-augmented large language model connected to instrument functions through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). The framework consists of 3 MCP-based agents: AFM Messenger converts natural-language instructions into checked instrument commands; AFM Pilot assesses image quality through a large language model (LLM) and, if necessary, adapts imaging parameters; and AFM Doctor diagnoses image artifacts and applies transparent post-processing from a pre-approved tool set. Because the language model performs image assessment rather than a fixed scalar objective or external optimizer, the same strategy can be applied across sample types and imaging modes without specific retraining. Safe hardware operation is enforced through an ambiguity check layer before execution. Benchmarking against fine-tuned and off-the-shelf tool-using models shows that this guarded execution layer, rather than model capability alone, reduces wrong-command execution to zero. In live experiments on different samples, AFM Pilot matched expert operators in image quality, iteration count, and tuning time, with no significant difference. These results demonstrate a safe route to agentic operation of scientific instruments, where experimental intent remains human-defined while command execution, image-based tuning, and post-processing are delegated to AI agents.

</details>


### [265] [Benchmarking AI Agents for Hardware Design Automation via MCP Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26199)
*Leonardo Liparulo,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究本地部署大语言模型在硬件设计工作流自动化中的可靠性，构建了基于MCP协议的基准测试，发现模型性能高度依赖任务结构和配置，全面工具描述能显著降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对嵌入式系统开发中因保密性约束无法使用托管专有API的问题，探索利用本地部署的大语言模型可靠地自动化专家定义的、具有依赖关系的硬件设计工作流。

Method: 构建一个复现专有硬件设计工具状态和依赖逻辑的Model Context Protocol (MCP) 服务器，建立涵盖单步编辑、多步依赖链、无效请求等场景的基准测试；评估七种开源模型，对比系统提示、工具描述细节、上下文范围及单/多智能体架构等配置策略。

Result: 强模型可实现接近完整的预期调用覆盖率，但可靠性受任务结构和配置影响显著：全面的工具描述一致减少故障；少样本提示可能导致部分模型严重失活；累积上下文损害受限模型；多智能体分解有助于弱模型或长会话，但会增加调用成本。

Conclusion: 为在状态敏感型硬件设计环境中部署本地LLM智能体提供了实践指导，强调了优化提示工程与架构选择对提升自动化可靠性的关键作用。

Abstract: We ask whether AI agents powered by locally deployed large language models can reliably automate expert-defined hardware design workflows in an industry-realistic tool-calling setting. In these environments, engineers issue repetitive, dependency-ordered operations---such as creating components, adding ports, and wiring connections---through specialised tools. Confidentiality constraints on component specifications and naming conventions often preclude hosted proprietary APIs, motivating the use of locally deployed models. To study this setting, we build a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server that reproduces the state and dependency logic of a proprietary hardware design tool used in embedded system development and construct a benchmark covering single-operation edits, multi-step dependency chains, invalid requests, misspelled prompts, and multi-server tool contexts. We evaluate seven open-source models comparing pipeline choices including system prompts, tool-description detail, context scope, and single-agent versus multi-agent architectures. Results show that strong models can achieve near-complete expected-call coverage on the benchmarked workflows, but reliability depends strongly on both task structure and agent configuration. Comprehensive tool descriptions consistently reduce failures, few-shot prompting can cause severe inaction for some models, cumulative context harms constrained models, and multi-agent decomposition helps weak workers or long sessions at the cost of additional calls. These findings provide practical guidance for deploying local LLM agents in stateful hardware design environments.

</details>


### [266] [GameWAM: A World Action Model for Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26200)
*Yuncheng Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GameWAM，首个用于原生闭环游戏和GUI控制的World-Action Model (WAM)，通过并行视觉和行动生成过程实现未来视觉观测与可执行键鼠轨迹的联合生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏智能体缺乏显式的世界动力学建模，而交互式世界模型又无法直接作为任务策略；WAM旨在统一这两个目标，但在视频游戏的动态性和开放交互中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1. 采用块因果条件（block-causal conditioning）和流匹配（flow matching）进行并行视觉和行动生成；2. 构建同步的游戏玩法和GUI轨迹以支持联合学习；3. 预测 gameplay/GUI 模式并采用特定分布和连续动作归一化处理异构原生控制；4. 引入块循环控制（block-cycle control）处理长视野交互，结合细粒度上下文和分层跨周期历史保持时间连续性。

Result: 实验显示该方法在任务成功率上具有竞争力，且执行的 native actions 数量少于对比智能体；同时发现了低频动作源印记（LASI）现象，揭示了生成式控制中的源敏感性故障模式。

Conclusion: GameWAM成功实现了原生闭环游戏控制，为理解生成式控制中的动力学建模与行动生成提供了新视角，并指出了当前方法在源敏感性方面的潜在缺陷。

Abstract: Modern video games combine first-person perception, rapid visual changes, persistent world state, and heterogeneous native controls. Existing game agents map visual and task context directly to actions but lack explicit world dynamics modeling, whereas interactive game world models predict visual futures from supplied actions but do not serve as task policies. World-Action Models (WAMs) unify these objectives, but remain largely unexplored under the dynamics and open-ended interaction of video games. We introduce GameWAM, to our knowledge the first WAM for native closed-loop gameplay and GUI control. GameWAM jointly generates future visual observations and executable keyboard-mouse trajectories through parallel visual and action generative processes with block-causal conditioning and flow matching. To support joint world-action learning, we construct synchronized gameplay and GUI trajectories. To handle heterogeneous native control, GameWAM predicts a gameplay/GUI mode at each action step and generates actions with mode-specific prediction distributions and continuous-action normalization. For long-horizon interaction, block-cycle control predicts beyond the committed horizon, executes only a short action prefix, and replans from new observations, while fine-grained within-cycle context and hierarchical cross-cycle history preserve temporal continuity. Experiments demonstrate competitive task success with fewer executed native actions than the compared agents. We further uncover Low-Frequency Action Source Imprinting (LASI), in which low-frequency components of the sampled action source systematically steer coarse generated camera motion under fixed conditioning, revealing a source-sensitivity failure mode in generative control. Project page is available at https://yunncheng.github.io/GameWAM/.

</details>


### [267] [Same Model, Different Harness: Different Coding-Agent Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26218)
*Sydney Lewis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证实，在固定模型和任务的情况下，优化编码智能体的“控制框架（harness）”能显著提升性能。通过压缩旧工具结果以节省上下文窗口，不仅提高了代码通过率，还减少了提示词消耗，表明评估时应将模型与控制框架视为一个整体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在模型权重和任务保持不变的前提下，改变编码智能体的控制框架（即决定模型视野、工具使用及工作流程的机制）是否会影响最终结果。

Method: 对比同一控制框架的两种配置：对照组按时间顺序提供完整对话历史；实验组在上下文填充时机械性缩短旧工具结果，并应对重复或停滞的工作。在SWE-bench Verified/Pro、FeatureBench等三个编码基准上进行测试，涵盖不同上下文窗口大小（紧密与宽泛）及多个不同设计的模型。

Result: 1. 在紧密上下文窗口下，实验组显著提升了平均任务失败-通过比例（F2PF，从28%升至49%）和完整解决方案数量（从43增至72）。
2. 无需针对模型微调，该处理方案对另外三种不同设计的模型也提升了各项指标。
3. 在宽上下文窗口下，Verified和Pro表现相近，但FeatureBench中实验组仍保持更高的F2PF，且每轮提示词令牌数更少。

Conclusion: 控制框架的改变直接影响了未修改权重的模型所能达成的效果。因此，在编码智能体评估中，应将模型和控制框架作为一个整体的被测求解器（solver）来对待，而非单独评估模型。

Abstract: A coding agent combines a model with a harness, which decides what the model sees, which tools it can use, and how the work continues. We ask whether changing the harness changes the result when the model and task stay fixed. We compare two configurations of the same harness on three coding benchmarks. The control supplies the full conversation in time order, while the treatment keeps the same record but mechanically shortens older tool results as the context fills and responds to repeated or stalled work.
  Under tight context, the treatment raises mean per-task fail-to-pass fraction (F2PF) in all three pressure comparisons and increases complete solutions on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro. The tight-window Verified comparison uses 169 tasks, a 20,480-token window, and a fixed 480-second attempt endpoint; on this cohort, treatment raises mean per-task F2PF from 28 percent to 49 percent and complete solutions from 43 to 72. Without model-specific retuning, the same frozen treatment also raises both endpoints on the same cohort for three additional models with different designs. In the wide-window Qwen3.6 comparisons, observed arm outcomes are close on Verified and Pro, while FeatureBench retains a higher mean per-task F2PF under treatment. On the wide-window Verified cohort, treatment also serves fewer prompt tokens per turn. Because changing the harness changed what unchanged model weights could accomplish, coding-agent evaluations should treat the model and harness together as the tested solver.

</details>


### [268] [Agent Mesh: Reliability Primitives for Non-Idempotent Agent Delegation - Identity Adequacy and Evidence Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26225)
*Mazhar Shaikh,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde,Harshal Pathak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过147起生产环境事故，揭示了服务网格原语（重试、超时、熔断）在自治代理场景下的失效机制，提出了以“委托”为执行单元的七种新型可靠性原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自治代理在编排器控制下执行受限软件任务时，传统服务网格机制因假设不成立而导致的性能损耗和故障放大问题。

Method: 对包含81次运行、具有成本测量和变异证明的生产级代理软件交付平台进行故障研究，分析147起编号事故的根因及后果。

Result: 发现重试、超时和错误率熔断三大基础假设均被违反；识别出身份充分性（Identity Adequacy）和证据充分性（Evidence Adequacy）两大根本原因；量化了误触发、死循环及正确工作被阻断等具体故障模式。

Conclusion: 传统的基于消息的可靠性机制在代理场景中失效，应转向以“委托”而非“消息”为强制执行单元的新型可靠性原语设计。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly perform bounded software tasks under an orchestrator that retries, resumes, and budgets them. The machinery such orchestrators reach for is the service mesh's: retry, timeout, and error-rate circuit breaking. We report a failure study of a production agentic software-delivery platform over 147 numbered incidents spanning 81 runs, each with a measured cost and, in most cases, a mutation proof reproducing the failure. All three assumptions those primitives rest on are violated in practice, and we quantify the consequences: a loop of fifty-four consecutive successful tool calls no error-rate breaker could see; a progress signal constant by construction, guaranteeing a false trip on the third repair round and driving one run from six of six components to three; twenty-one events accumulated across six invocations of one delegation, making a correct, idempotent component unwinnable; a misrouted failure that woke five components for a two-component fault, leaving three bystanders regressing working code; and twelve incidents in which the enforcement layer blocked correct work, the most expensive costing 107 agent turns and zero accepted writes. We find one cross-cutting cause and its dual. Identity adequacy: in five separate subsystems an identity that failed to discriminate produced a confident wrong answer, and two of them derived the corrective rule independently. Evidence adequacy: a reliability decision may be taken only on evidence capable of moving, attributable to what it measures, and deterministic under identical conditions. From the findings we derive seven reliability primitives whose enforcement unit is the delegation rather than the message, and specify the controlled evaluation the study motivates but does not constitute.

</details>


### [269] [LLM Agents for Time-Series: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26226)
*Yilong Chen,Xiao Qin,Chenghao Liu,Liang Wu,Noelle I. Samia,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述提出了一种以问题为导向的分类法，将基于大语言模型（LLM）的时间序列智能体系统分为四类：预测与推理、增强与合成、异常检测与诊断、决策支持。通过考察任务需求如何塑造智能体的架构、工具使用和记忆设计，并对比现有数据集和性能，为时间序列领域的LLM智能体设计提供了任务导向指南及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体在时间序列问题中的应用日益增多，但不同任务设置下的设计选择差异巨大，缺乏统一的系统性梳理。旨在解决这一现状，提供一种基于问题解决而非孤立技术组件的分类视角，以指导系统设计。

Method: 采用以问题为导向的分类法（problem-driven taxonomy），将现有系统划分为四大类别：预测与推理、增强与合成、异常检测与诊断、决策支持。深入分析每类系统中任务需求对智能体架构、工具使用和记忆设计的影响。同时总结代表性数据集和环境，并在共享或相近设置下比较模型性能。

Result: 建立了针对时间序列问题的LLM智能体任务导向分类体系；揭示了任务需求与智能体设计要素（架构、工具、记忆）之间的关联；汇总了相关数据集与环境；对比了各系统在相似条件下的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该综述为设计面向时间序列问题的LLM智能体提供了系统的任务导向指南，并指出了当前研究中存在的开放性问题及未来工作方向。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being developed for time-series problems, but their design choices vary substantially across task settings. This survey adopts a problem-driven taxonomy that organizes these systems by the time-series problems they address rather than by isolated technical components. We group existing systems into four categories: forecasting and reasoning, augmentation and synthesis, anomaly detection and diagnosis, and decision support. Within each category, we examine how task requirements shape agent architecture, tool use, and memory design. We further summarize representative datasets and environments, and compare reported model performance under shared or closely related settings. Overall, this survey offers a task-oriented guide to designing LLM-based agents for time-series problems and identifies open gaps for future work.

</details>


### [270] [The Reasoning Tax: Token Economics of LLM Reasoning Across Task Types and Deployment Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26235)
*Sachin Gopal Wani,Ajay Dholakia,David Ellison*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Token Economy Score (TES)指标，评估推理模型在生成额外思考token时的边际准确率增益。研究发现任务结构比难度更能预测推理效率，且存在收益递减现象，建议根据任务类型、努力程度和部署上下文选择性启用推理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有仅关注准确率的基准测试无法回答的核心部署问题：扩展的推理token何时能证明其成本是合理的？

Method: 引入边际基准测试指标Token Economy Score (TES)，衡量推理模型相对于非推理基线的准确率增益与生成token倍数的比值。在涵盖数学、代码生成等七个基准上的151次评估运行中进行实证分析，考察任务结构、推理努力程度及部署语境三个维度。

Result: 1. 任务结构（如序列推理链）比名义难度更有效地预测推理效率；2. 高推理努力水平下存在系统性收益递减，甚至导致准确率下降；3. Reasoning Cost Share (RCS)显示推理支出常被内部思考主导；4. Deployment Cost Multiplier (DCM)揭示了本地部署对经济性的影响。

Conclusion: 支持基于基准测试的模型选择规则：应根据任务类型、努力水平和部署上下文选择性启用推理功能，而非将其视为普遍有益的模式。

Abstract: Accuracy-only benchmarking of reasoning-capable large language models misses a central deployment question: when do extended thinking tokens earn their cost? We introduce the Token Economy Score (TES), a marginal benchmarking metric that measures the accuracy gain of a reasoning model over a non-reasoning baseline, normalized by the generated-token multiplier. We define paired and approximated TES variants for model families with reasoning toggles and frontier models without direct non-reasoning counterparts. We then conduct an empirical benchmarking analysis across 151 model-benchmark evaluation runs on seven benchmarks spanning mathematics, code generation, science reasoning, instruction following, expert knowledge, knowledge recall, and research-level physics. The analysis examines three deployment-facing dimensions: which task structures yield positive marginal reasoning efficiency, how increasing reasoning effort changes TES within model families, and how deployment context changes economic viability. Results show that task structure predicts reasoning efficiency better than nominal difficulty: sequential inferencechain tasks such as AIME 2025 and LiveCodeBench show high TES, while knowledge-recall tasks such as MMLU-Pro show low TES despite their difficulty. We also find systematic diminishing returns at higher reasoning effort levels, including cases where additional thinking reduces accuracy. Finally, Reasoning Cost Share (RCS) shows that inference spend is often dominated by internal thinking, while Deployment Cost Multiplier (DCM) shows how on-premises deployment can change the economics of otherwise costly reasoning workloads. These findings support a benchmarking-driven model-selection rule: enable reasoning selectively by task type, effort level, and deployment context rather than treating it as a universally beneficial mode.

</details>


### [271] [6.5% of the Neuro-Symbolic Literature Can Be Reproduced from Its Published Artifacts, a Six-Stage Audit Framework and First Instantiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26236)
*Brandon Colelough,Vladimir Martirosyan,Ishan Tamrakar,William Regli,Aditya Kumar,Anh N. Nhu,Dhruv Dubey,Raj Ambavane,Haowei Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实例化了一个针对神经符号AI（NSAI）领域的六阶段可重复性审计框架。通过对5,497条记录的系统筛选，最终对455项研究进行了尝试复现，仅成功复现了85项（占eligible corpus的6.52%）。研究发现大量复现失败源于缺失非代码类工件或不可用的代码仓库，揭示了即使声明“代码可用”仍存在严重的可重复性赤字，呼吁强制要求提交完整、版本控制且永久归档的工件包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学研究文献中科学主张的可重复性面临挑战，特别是在神经符号AI（NSAI）子领域，需要一种系统化的方法来审计和量化这一现象，以改善学术出版的透明度和可靠性。

Method: 构建并应用一个六阶段审计框架：1) 检索记录；2) 标题/摘要筛选；3) 全文筛选及寻找公开代码工件；4-5) 对拥有工件的记录进行有界重运行（bounded rerun）；6) 综合评估。具体实施中，从5,497条初始记录中筛选出1,304项符合条件的记录，其中455项进入复现阶段，最终完全或部分复现了85项研究。

Result: 在尝试复现的455项研究中，仅有85项被成功完全或部分复现（成功率6.52%）。主要障碍包括：321次尝试因缺失非代码工件受阻，42次因缺失或不可用的代码仓库受阻。这表明即使作者声明代码可用，仍存在显著的可重复性缺口。

Conclusion: 当前NSAI领域的实证论文存在严重的可重复性危机，现有的“代码可用”声明不足以保障复现。建议未来NSAI出版物应强制要求在投稿时提供完整、经过版本控制且永久归档的工件包（artifact bundles），以提升研究的可重复性和透明度。

Abstract: We present a six-stage framework for auditing the reproducibility of scientific claims across a research literature within the computer science domain, and instantiate our framework for the neuro-symbolic AI (NSAI) subdomain. Instantiating the framework on the NSAI subdomain produced a multi-year audit. Stage one retrieved 5,497 records and removed 3,018 duplicates. Stage two screened the 2,479 unique records at title and abstract, identifying 1,365 self-identified NSAI records, then removed a further 61 at full text for off-topic, non-research, no-quantitative-evaluation, or inaccessible-full-text reasons. Stage three sought a verifiable public code artifact for each of the 1,304 eligible records and found none for 849, leaving 455 to enter the artifact inventory and bounded rerun of stages four and five. We fully or partially reproduced 85 studies, 6.52% of the eligible corpus and 18.68% of attempted reruns. We found that 321 attempted reruns were blocked by missing non- code artifacts and 42 by missing or unusable code repositories. These figures quantify a persistent reproducibility deficit that survives even nominal "code available" declarations, and signal the need for enforced, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles in future NSAI publications. We argue that empirical NSAI papers should be required at submission time to provide complete, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles.

</details>


### [272] [SKILL.state: Scalable Long-Horizon Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26263)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari,Jonghyun Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILL.state 提出了一种新的运行时架构，通过用显式、可变的执行状态替代传统的追加式对话历史，解决了大语言模型在长程任务中因上下文增长导致的延迟和性能下降问题，显著提升了准确率并降低了 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体运行时依赖不断增长的对话历史来维持执行，这导致在长周期任务中出现延迟增加（latency degradation）和上下文污染（context-poisoning）失败的问题。

Method: 提出 SKILL.state 架构，在每个执行步骤中，模型仅接收不可变技能规范、当前结构化执行状态和最新观测值；中间推理过程在生成经过验证的状态更新后立即被丢弃，从而防止提示词随执行历史无限增长。

Result: 在多样化的数据集、模型和执行环境中，SKILL.state 提高了任务准确率，同时大幅减少了累积 token 消耗。

Conclusion: 显式执行状态是一种有效且与架构无关的抽象方法，适用于可扩展的长周期智能体技能执行。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents executing complex, long-running procedural skills. Existing agent runtimes maintain execution by continually appending observations, actions, and intermediate reasoning traces to an ever-growing conversation history, causing latency degradation and context-poisoning failures over long horizons. We present SKILL.state, a runtime architecture that replaces append-only conversational history with an explicit, mutable execution state. At each execution step, the model receives only the immutable skill specification, the current structured execution state, and the latest observation. Intermediate reasoning is discarded immediately after producing a validated state update, preventing prompt growth with execution history. Across diverse datasets, models, and execution environments, SKILL.state improves task accuracy while substantially reducing cumulative token consumption. Our results demonstrate that explicit execution state is an effective and architecture-agnostic abstraction for scalable long-horizon agent skills.

</details>


### [273] [Assessing mentalization in humans and large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26291)
*Aamir Sohail,Xintong Zhong,Arkady Konovalov,Patricia L. Lockwood,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过经济博弈和计算建模，揭示大型语言模型（LLMs）具备心智化能力，其中GPT-5在应对复杂对手时表现出优于人类的递归推理灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在理论-of-mind任务中表现与人类一致，但其是否能通过心智化引导适应性行为尚不明确，需进一步探究其潜在策略及与人类的比较智能。

Method: 使用两个经济博弈结合认知计算建模，测试DeepSeek、GPT-4.1、GPT-5和Gemini 2.0 Flash共2,099个LLM代理，对比不同复杂度的对手及提示策略效果，并以251名人类参与者作为基准进行比较。

Result: LLMs显示出明显的心智化行为和计算特征，且因模型提供商和规模而异；战略性提示通常通过诱导更复杂的推理提升性能，但提升幅度因任务而异；GPT-5能灵活调整递归推理深度，性能优于人类。

Conclusion: 不同LLMs在心智化能力上存在差异，认知计算建模是评估人机比较智能的有效形式化方法。

Abstract: Mentalization - the ability to infer others' beliefs and intentions to guide one's own choices - is a key cognitive function underlying human social interactions. Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate behaviour consistent with humans on theory-of-mind tasks, yet whether these models can guide adaptive behaviour through mentalization is unknown. Here we use two economic games with cognitive computational modeling to uncover the latent strategies underlying mentalization in LLMs. We tested individual LLM agents across four model families, DeepSeek, GPT-4.1, GPT-5 and Gemini 2.0 Flash (N = 2,099), against opponents of varying sophistication and examined whether a prompting strategy designed to elicit strategic reasoning improved performance. We benchmarked results against human participants (N = 251) as a comparative measure. Across both games, LLMs showed clear behavioural and computational signatures of mentalizing that differed markedly by model provider and size. Strategic prompting generally improved performance by inducing more sophisticated reasoning, yet the extent of the benefit differed across the two tasks. Last, GPT-5 agents flexibly adapted their recursive depth of reasoning to increasingly sophisticated opponents, demonstrating superior performance to human participants. Collectively, we demonstrate different capacities for mentalization across LLMs, and highlight cognitive computational modeling as a formal method for assessing comparative intelligence across humans and machines.

</details>


### [274] [Approved Too Late: Verdict Staleness in LLM-Guarded Self-Adaptive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26306)
*Ilai Shraga,Roei Eshel,Lior Gorelik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型（LLM）护栏在自适应系统（SAS）中因执行延迟导致的“检查-使用”时间差（TOCTOU）漏洞，提出了“新鲜度受限盾牌”（FBS）。该方法通过估算安全边距和特征波动性来预测审批的有效期限，显著降低了审批过期率，并确立了审批需同时满足检查时正确和使用时无效性的新鲜度契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM护栏在自适应系统中面临的执行阶段“检查-使用”时间差（TOCTOU）风险。即护栏在检查时刻给出的批准可能在执行时刻已失效，导致安全隐患。研究旨在评估判决的新鲜度及其在不同场景下的有效性变化。

Method: 1. 定义三种衡量判决新鲜度的指标：固定动作重放下的全候选判决变更、记录闭环轨迹上的Oracle标记批准过期、以及法官条件使用时的无效性。
2. 提出“新鲜度受限盾牌”（FBS），一种无需显式植物动力学模型的机制，通过分析安全侧边距和近期特征波动性来估计每个批准的 validity horizon（有效期限）。
3. 在五个可复现的SAS环境中进行实验验证。
4. 对四个LLM法官进行独立审计。

Result: 1. 在8个模拟器步骤的重放偏移下，全候选判决变更率为5.3%-48.4%。
2. FBS将Oracle标记的批准过期率从3.4%-24.7%大幅降低至0-1.8%。
3. 审计发现所有批准流中都存在非零的法官条件使用时无效性。

Conclusion: 确立了“新鲜度契约”，要求LLM护栏的每一次批准不仅要在检查时刻正确，还必须在实际使用时刻保持有效。FBS能有效缓解TOCTOU危害，提升系统在动态环境中的安全性。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) guardrail for a self-adaptive system (SAS) may issue an approval that is correct at check time but stale by actuation. This creates an Execute-stage time-of-check to time-of-use (TOCTOU) hazard. We study verdict freshness: whether a guardrail verdict remains valid when used. We distinguish three quantities that answer different questions: all-candidate verdict change under fixed-action replay, oracle-labeled approval expiry on recorded closed-loop trajectories, and judge-conditioned use-time invalidity. Across five reproducible SAS environments, all-candidate verdict-change rates span 5.3-48.4% at a common replay shift of eight simulator steps. We introduce the Freshness-Bounded Shield (FBS), which estimates each approval's validity horizon from its safe-side margin and recent feature volatility, without an explicit plant-dynamics model. Using fixed settings documented in the artifact, FBS reduces oracle-labeled approval-expiry rates from 3.4-24.7% to 0-1.8% at the same shift. A separate audit of four LLM judges finds nonzero judge-conditioned use-time invalidity in every approval stream. We formulate a freshness contract: every approval must be correct at check time and remain valid at use time.

</details>


### [275] [FaithSieve: Fine-Grained Evaluation of Math Proofs with Faithful Formal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26310)
*Ziyu Wang,Qiming Dai,Yishan Wu,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FaithSieve框架，结合Lean形式化验证与语义对齐评分，对自然语言数学证明进行细粒度评估和首错定位，显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型生成数学证明时，难以可靠判定正确性及定位早期逻辑错误；传统基于自然语言的评估易忽略局部推理缺口，而直接使用形式化证明器存在局部性缺失和语义漂移问题。

Method: 引入FaithSieve框架：将粗糙证明步骤分解为局部推理单元，提取类型化证明义务，并通过形式化评估代理验证；利用语义对齐评分作为门控机制，仅在形式化陈述忠实保留原始上下文、对象和逻辑形式时才纳入Lean证据。

Result: 在ProofLoc-Olympiad数据集（350题）上，使用GPT-5.4骨干的FaithSieve实现81.43%的首错精确准确率，优于基线72.29%；在ProofLoc-University基准（200题，6个高级领域）上达到84.5%，优于基线75.0%。

Conclusion: 通过将证明分解为细粒度单元并用忠实的形式化证据 grounding，显著提高了自然语言推理评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models can now generate complex, multi-step mathematical proofs, but reliably determining their correctness and localizing early logical errors remains a critical challenge. Existing evaluation approaches largely depend on model-based natural-language judgments, which often overlook local reasoning gaps. While formal theorem provers like Lean offer a path to rigorous verification, using them to evaluate informal text requires solving locality and semantic mismatches: a prover might bypass a local flaw by proving an overly broad target, or validate an auto-formalized statement that drifts from the original mathematical intent. To address this, we introduce FaithSieve, a Lean-assisted framework for fine-grained evaluation of natural-language mathematical proofs. FaithSieve decomposes coarse proof steps into local reasoning units, extracts typed proof obligations, and verifies them through a formal evaluation agent. Formal validation is gated by semantic alignment scoring, so Lean evidence is incorporated only when the formal statement faithfully preserves the context, objects, and logical form of the original claim. We construct two expert-verified datasets, ProofLoc-Olympiad and ProofLoc-University, to benchmark first-error localization. On the 350-problem Olympiad dataset, FaithSieve using a GPT-5.4 backbone achieves 81.43% exact first-error accuracy, outperforming the direct-judging baseline of 72.29%. Furthermore, on the 200-problem ProofLoc-University benchmark spanning six advanced domains, FaithSieve reaches 84.5% exact accuracy, compared to 75.0% for the direct judge. Our work demonstrates that decomposing proofs into fine-grained units and grounding them with faithful formal evidence significantly improves reliable evaluation of natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [276] [ProofEvolve: Neuro-Symbolic Evolution for Formal Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26334)
*Wenqian Ye,Ziwei Guan,Eric Xie,Bohan Liu,Shivani Modi,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProofEvolve框架，通过神经符号结合演化显式符号证明结构，解决现有方法无法有效利用部分证明经验和缺乏递归自改进能力的问题，在Lean基准测试中取得最高求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经定理证明器未能充分保留递归自改进结构：要么通过昂贵的权重更新嵌入经验，要么仅在当前问题内保留中间推导；且过度依赖稀疏的全程证明反馈，忽略了未成功但包含有用发现的局部尝试。

Method: 提出ProofEvolve神经符号框架：1) 神经网络提议变异算子（分解、修复、模式重组）；2) Lean内核验证每个证明转换；3) 演化闭环计算有向无环图(DAGs)的验证闭包；4) 在行为索引档案中演化部分AND-OR证明DAGs；5) 跨问题通过内核检查的模式提取将新证明子DAGs加入持久模式库；6) 通过类型化模式重组继承已解结果，暴露剩余前提为新子目标。

Result: 在三个竞争级别的Lean基准测试中，ProofEvolve实现了被评估证明系统中最高的平均求解率。

Conclusion: 该演化过程保留了不完整尝试中的验证结果，使其可用于后续证明而不削弱形式正确性，有效扩展了知识边界并实现了真正的递归自改进。

Abstract: Automated theorem proving offers a natural foundation for recursive self-improvement in scientific discovery. However, existing neural provers do not fully preserve this recursive structure, where the learning process should be self-improving over time. Existing methods either embed proof experience into model parameters through expensive weight updates, or keep verified intermediate deductions only within the current problem. In addition, these methods also heavily rely on sparse whole-proof feedback, even when unsuccessful partial attempts contain useful discoveries. To close the gap, we propose ProofEvolve, a neuro-symbolic framework that evolves explicit, formally verified symbolic proof structures with neural models to decisively expand the knowledge boundary. In this framework, the neural model proposes variation operators, including decompositions, repairs, and schema recombinations. The symbolic Lean kernel verifies every proof transition. Over the evolution loops, ProofEvolve computes verified closure over the resulting proof directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). Within each problem, ProofEvolve evolves partial AND-OR proof DAGs in a behaviorally indexed archive. Across problems, kernel-checked schema extraction adds newly proved sub-DAGs to a persistent schema library. Proof DAGs inherit the solved results through typed schema recombination, with every residual premise exposed as a new subgoal. This evolutionary process preserves verified results from incomplete attempts and makes them available for later proofs without weakening formal soundness. Across three competition-level Lean benchmarks, ProofEvolve achieves the highest average solve rate among the evaluated proof systems.

</details>


### [277] [Fine-Tuning of Transformer models with Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26430)
*Harshavardhan Adepu,Li Zhang,Sanjiv Kumar,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FrameFT，通过融合帧基下的稀疏系数矩阵更新参数，显著降低PEFT内存开销和可训练参数量，同时保持或超越现有SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调（PEFT）方法（如LoRA）的内存需求随模型隐藏维度$d$和秩$r$线性增长（$\mathcal{O}(dr)$），限制了其在大规模模型上的效率。

Method: 将参数更新$\Delta W$建模为融合帧（Fusion Frame）基下的稀疏系数矩阵。利用算法生成的、可在层间共享的融合帧，仅存储和优化稀疏系数，从而减少内存占用并利用稀疏性提升计算效率。

Result: 在语言任务为主的监督微调基准及视觉模型应用中，FrameFT在性能上达到或超过当前最先进的PEFT技术，但所需可训练参数量大幅减少；理论分析提供了收敛性保证。

Conclusion: FrameFT通过融合帧基稀疏表示有效解决了PEFT内存扩展问题，实现了高性能与低资源消耗的统一。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) strategies such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) are effective solutions for fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models; however, their memory requirements scale with the size of the model, $\mathcal{O}(dr)$, where $d$ is the model's hidden dimension and $r$ is the rank. Our proposal, FrameFT, models the parameter update $ΔW$ with a sparse coefficient matrix in a Fusion Frame basis. Fusion Frames can be generated algorithmically and shared across model layers, enabling very efficient updates. Only the sparse coefficients of the basis expansion are stored/optimized, reducing the memory footprint. The sparse structure of the coefficient matrix in FrameFT and the sparsity in the Fusion Frames give large compute benefits, and our analysis provides formal convergence results. We evaluate the idea across a suite of supervised fine-tuning benchmarks, focusing on language tasks, but also report application to vision models. Our experiments show that FrameFT achieves performance on par with/exceeding state-of-the-art PEFT techniques, but needs far fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [278] [Don't Overthink, Don't Underthink: Toward Adaptive Reasoning in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26442)
*Md Jueal Mia,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前大模型在智能体工作流中缺乏动态推理能力，导致过度或不足推理。通过评估发现过度推理增加成本无益于准确率，而推理不足导致错误。主张未来研究应聚焦于根据任务需求自适应分配推理资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖固定的推理控制（如固定token预算），无法适应智能体系统中动态变化的推理需求（如规划、工具使用等），导致计算浪费或解决方案不完整。

Method: 在MATH-500和GAIA基准上评估，通过分析工具决策延迟、token消耗、token限制耗尽情况及答案正确性，识别并量化“过度推理”和“推理不足”两种失败模式。

Result: 过度推理与高昂的计算成本相关，但未带来相应的准确率提升；推理不足则一致地与不正确或不完整的解决方案相关联。

Conclusion: 下一代智能体AI的主要挑战在于如何根据动态任务需求自适应地分配推理资源，而非仅仅关注推理量，需开发自适应推理机制。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown that increased inference-time reasoning can improve performance on complex tasks. However, many existing approaches rely on fixed or preallocated reasoning controls, such as fixed token budgets, pre-execution difficulty estimates, or activation-space interventions, and are often evaluated on standalone reasoning benchmarks rather than full agentic workflows. These assumptions may not hold in agentic AI systems, where reasoning requirements evolve dynamically through planning, tool use, memory retrieval, and agent-to-agent interactions. Consequently, reasoning can become either excessive or insufficient, resulting in unnecessary computation, increased latency, planning drift, excessive tool use, or incomplete solutions. We argue that a major challenge for next-generation agentic AI is not merely how much reasoning a language model should perform, but how it should allocate reasoning according to evolving task demands. We characterize over-reasoning and under-reasoning as recurring failure modes of misallocated reasoning and evaluate them on MATH-500 and the GAIA public validation benchmark. Using tool-decision latency, token consumption, token-limit exhaustion, and answer correctness, our results suggest that cases classified as over-reasoning are associated with higher computational cost without proportional accuracy gains, whereas cases classified as under-reasoning are consistently associated with incorrect or incomplete solutions. These findings motivate future research on adaptive reasoning mechanisms for agentic AI.

</details>


### [279] [PILOT in the Loop: Live Self-Improvement for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26530)
*Yang Xiao,Yusong Sun,Haoyi Wu,Wenyang Hui,Wen Da,Zhaokai Luo,Mu Chuan,Yao Hu,Wenjie Li,Chengyue Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PILOT框架，通过“实时引导”和“实时自我进化”机制实现智能体的在线自我改进，解决现有方法无法在执行中动态调整的问题，显著提升性能并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自改进方法多为离线处理经验，无法在执行过程中重定向当前任务或立即验证所学教训；现有架构（如单代理自我修正或子代理委托）无法完全支持这种实时的、持续的学习与调整需求。

Method: 提出PILOT监督者-工作者框架，包含两个耦合机制：(1) 实时引导：独立监督者可在执行期间重定向或中止活跃的工作者；(2) 实时自我进化：将执行过程中揭示的流程和失败模式提炼为可复用的技能和记忆，更新持久化 harness。

Result: 在两个固定骨干模型和三个基准测试中，PILOT在六项配置中有五项排名第一。在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，相比其他harnesses提升高达9.8个百分点；在自改进设置下，配合GLM-5.1和Kimi-K2.6分别提升14.6和12.4分。输出token减少约43%-47%，每百万token的成功评估率提升超110%。

Conclusion: PILOT证明了在线自我改进的有效性，能够显著增强智能体在当前及未来任务中的表现，同时大幅优化资源效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon agent runs generate experience that can improve both the current run and future work. Most self-improvement methods process this experience only after execution ends, so they cannot redirect the active run or immediately apply and validate lessons learned from it. We argue that self-improvement should instead be live, using emerging experience both to redirect the active run and to update the persistent harness. Existing agent architectures do not fully support this goal. Single-agent self-correction combines task execution and trajectory assessment within one context, while subagent delegation separates execution but typically cannot redirect an active subagent. We present PILOT, a supervisor-worker harness for live self-improvement through two coupled mechanisms: (1) live steering lets a separate supervisor redirect or abort the active worker during execution; and (2) live self-evolution distils procedures and failure modes revealed during execution into reusable skills and memory. Across two frozen backbones and three benchmarks, PILOT ranks first in five of six configurations. On Terminal-Bench 2.0, PILOT outperforms counterpart harnesses by up to 9.8 percentage points. In the self-improvement setting, PILOT gains 14.6 points with GLM-5.1 and 12.4 points with Kimi-K2.6. Mean output tokens fall by 42.9% and 47.4%, while successful evaluations per million output tokens rise by 110.3% and 134.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [280] [Multi2AV-Safety: Benchmarking Safety in Multimodal-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26535)
*Kaichao Jiang,Changtao Miao,Baiqi Wu,Zhiyuan Lu,Kang Yang,Peiwei Zhao,Junchi Chen,Yunfeng Diao,He Liu,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对多模态条件（文本/图像/音频/视频组合）的音视频生成安全基准Multi2AV-Safety，揭示现有安全护栏在跨模态组合风险感知上的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准主要关注单一提示词或固定接口，无法有效评估由多个良性或弱有害输入在多模态和时间维度上交互产生的组合性安全风险。

Method: 构建涵盖所有11种非单例T/I/A/V条件配置的安全基准，包含11,024个攻击实例，并对代表性多模态安全护栏进行评估。

Result: 发现两类互补的失效模式：一是单独良性的输入组合后产生有害语义；二是显式有害线索在混合良性多模态上下文时更难检测。

Conclusion: 当前安全护栏缺乏“组合风险感知”能力，无法可靠地跨模态和时间整合安全证据，这是保护多模态条件音视频生成的核心能力缺口。

Abstract: Audio-video generation is rapidly moving from prompt-driven synthesis toward multimodal conditioning, where text, images, audio, and video can jointly shape the generated output. This shift changes the nature of safety evaluation: harmful intent may no longer reside in any single input, but instead emerge from how otherwise benign or weakly harmful conditions interact across modalities and time. Existing safety benchmarks, however, remain largely prompt-centric or tied to fixed conditioning interfaces, leaving such compositional risks difficult to study systematically. To bridge this gap, we introduce Multi2AV-Safety, the first safety benchmark, to the best of our knowledge, to cover all 11 non-singleton T/I/A/V conditioning configurations for audio-video generation, comprising 11,024 attack instances. Evaluation on Multi2AV-Safety reveals systematic weaknesses in representative multimodal safety guards across attack mechanisms and harm-evidence structures. Our evaluation reveals two complementary failure modes: harmful semantics can emerge from the combination of individually benign inputs, while explicit harmful cues can become harder to detect when mixed with benign multimodal context. Together, these results identify \emph{compositional risk perception} as a central capability gap in safeguarding multimodal-conditioned audio-video generation: current safety guards fail to reliably integrate safety evidence across modalities and time, even when all conditioning inputs are observable. The dataset will be publicly released in October 2026.

</details>


### [281] [DuMateBench: Evaluating Autonomous Agents in Complex Real-World Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26546)
*Zechun Niu,Yukun Zhao,Jiaxin Zhang,Xu Shen,Jinhua Si,Han Tian,Can Xu,Yunfan Song,Jiaxin Mao,Yansong Gao,Yuchen Li,Jianmin Wu,Lingyong Yan,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DuMateBench是一个基于真实用户会话重建的基准测试，旨在评估自主智能体在复杂、不稳定和嘈杂环境中的多工具工作流执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常将任务按应用或能力分离，且评估环境过于理想化（清洁、稳定），无法反映现实世界中智能体面临的复杂性和不稳定性。

Method: 从大规模生产平台收集并匿名化处理的用户会话，重建包含历史交互、配置和工作状态的任务；引入“不足、不稳定、噪声”三种环境复杂性；使用混合确定性及LLM-as-Judge协议进行评估。

Result: 涵盖200个任务、8种场景和17类能力；实验显示现有智能体框架与LLM组合在严格任务完成上存在显著差距；性能受LLM能力和智能体框架共同影响。

Conclusion: 真实世界的环境复杂性对智能体表现有重大影响，当前系统需提升鲁棒性；代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly adopted to complete complex, multi-tool workflows in real-world settings. However, existing benchmarks typically separate tasks by application or capability and evaluate agents in environments that are cleaner and more stable than those encountered in practice. We introduce DuMateBench, a real-session benchmark reconstructed from anonymized and privacy-screened user sessions collected from a large-scale production agent platform. Each task preserves the relevant pre-solution interaction history, persistent configurations, and workspace state, and is then validated through human verification. The resulting benchmark comprises 200 tasks spanning 8 broad scenarios and 17 fine-grained capability categories, with most tasks requiring multiple capability coordination. We execute these tasks in isolated Docker containers injected with three forms of real-world environmental complexity: Insufficient, Unstable, and Noisy, and assess performance using a hybrid deterministic and LLM-as-Judge evaluation protocol. Experiments across five representative autonomous-agent frameworks paired with four state-of-the-art LLMs reveal substantial gaps in strict task completion. Complementary robustness, efficiency, and diagnostic analyses further show that performance under environmental perturbations is jointly shaped by the capabilities of the LLM and the surrounding agent framework. The code and data are publicly available at https://dumatebench.com/.

</details>


### [282] [AgentJudgeBench: A Multi-Difficulty Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Judges on Agentic Tool-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26623)
*Abhigya Verma,Amit Kumar Saha,Seganrasan Subramanian,Sai Harshitha Aluru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个针对工作流DAG上代理工具调用的LLM-as-a-judge基准AgentJudgeBench，揭示模型规模无法克服由任务难度驱动的结构性性能上限，且真实标签暴露并非总是有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注开放式文本或偏好评估，缺乏对结构化、依赖驱动的工作流中LLM作为裁判可靠性的系统性考察。

Method: 构建包含3,808个实例的基准，涵盖6种DAG拓扑和3个难度层级；在有无真实标签条件下，使用5个生成器和6个裁判模型进行评估；测试思维链、温度调整及结构化评估准则等缓解策略。

Result: 1. 裁判一致性随难度单调下降（无真实标签时快1.5倍）；2. 困难查询下所有模型收敛至77-82%狭窄区间，显示存在结构性天花板；3. 真实标签暴露导致部分前沿模型（如GPT-5.4）对齐度下降（过度锚定）；4. 思维链无效，结构化准则提升有限且不通用；5. QwQ-32B在有标签时最匹配程序参考，GPT-OSS-120B最符合人类对齐。

Conclusion: 当前LLM裁判存在根本性局限，单纯增加模型容量无法突破由任务难度决定的性能瓶颈，需结合结构化评估与谨慎使用真实标签以优化代理系统评估。

Abstract: LLM judges are widely used to evaluate agentic tool-calling systems, yet their reliability on structured, dependency-driven workflows remains largely unexamined. We present AgentJudgeBench, the first benchmark to systematically study LLM-as-a-judge reliability for agentic tool-calling over workflow DAGs, as distinct from the broader LLM-as-a-judge task of open-ended text or preference evaluation. The benchmark comprises 3,808 instances spanning six DAG topologies and three difficulty tiers, evaluated with five generators (3B-70B open-weight models and GPT-5.4) and six judges (20B to frontier scale) under paired with- and without-ground-truth conditions. Judge alignment degrades monotonically with task difficulty, 1.5x faster without ground truth, and on hard queries without ground truth all six judges converge to a narrow 77-82% band regardless of scale, revealing a structural ceiling driven primarily by task difficulty, though its height is partly prompt-dependent for weaker generators, that model capacity alone cannot overcome. Ground-truth exposure is not uniformly beneficial: it reduces alignment for GPT-5.4 (1.5 pp) and Gemini-2.5-Pro (3.9 pp), consistent with over-anchoring. Among mitigation strategies, chain-of-thought reasoning and judge temperature both have negligible effect, while structured evaluation rubrics improve alignment by up to 6.5 pp but do not generalize uniformly across judge-generator pairs. With ground truth, QwQ-32B best matches the programmatic reference, while a human validation study identifies GPT-OSS-120B as the most human-aligned judge; without it, frontier judges lead only marginally within the shared ceiling. These results expose fundamental limitations of current LLM judges and yield practical guidelines for reliable evaluation in agentic systems.

</details>


### [283] [SIGMA: Structured Noise-Effect-Aware Grouped Multi-Agent Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26683)
*Li Mingqian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA框架，通过利用合作结构中的“结构化噪声效应”，在分层协作中实现多智能体强化学习（MARL）对观测噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒MARL方法未能显式刻画或利用由底层合作结构导致的“结构化噪声效应”（即噪声诱导的决策效应在任务相关性强时局部相关，而在不同代理间全局异质），导致在嘈杂观测下协调性不足。

Method: 提出SIGMA分层协作框架：1) 基于密度的分组将智能体组织为自适应局部结构；2) 组内共识聚合以保留共享任务信息并平滑个体偏差；3) 组间注意力机制自适应整合跨组信息，兼顾全局协调与异质性贡献。

Result: 在StarCraft II的嘈杂观测任务中验证了结构化噪声效应的存在；SIGMA在观测噪声下显著提升了鲁棒性，同时在无噪声环境中保持了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 显式利用合作结构中的噪声效应特征，能有效提升多智能体系统在嘈杂环境下的鲁棒协调能力。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces significant challenges in maintaining robust coordination under noisy observations. Although observation disturbances are often introduced independently across agents, their downstream effects on cooperative decision-making can become structured through underlying cooperation structures. We characterize this phenomenon as structured noise effects, where noise-induced decision effects exhibit local correlation among agents with stronger task-related dependencies while remaining globally heterogeneous across different agents and local structures. Existing robust MARL methods, however, rarely explicitly characterize or exploit such structure-dependent noise effects. To address this limitation, we propose SIGMA, a hierarchical collaboration framework that exploits cooperation structures to learn robust representations under noisy observations. SIGMA first organizes agents into adaptive local structures through density-based grouping and performs intra-group consensus aggregation to preserve shared task-relevant information while smoothing agent-specific representation deviations. Inter-group attention then adaptively integrates information across different groups to preserve global coordination while accommodating their heterogeneous contributions. Experiments on noisy-observation tasks in StarCraft II empirically validate the structured noise effects and demonstrate that SIGMA consistently improves robustness under observation noise while maintaining competitive performance in noise-free environments.

</details>


### [284] [Relational Over-Regularization: Graph-Based AI-Generated Text Detection via Sentence Transition Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26694)
*Hyeonchu Park,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于句子对级别结构信号（关系过度正则化 ROR）的AI生成文本检测方法，通过CSFG图框架实现高精度和强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖词级统计或独立风格特征，易过拟合特定生成器且在分布偏移下失效；需发现更鲁棒的结构性信号以解决跨模型检测难题。

Method: 形式化“关系过度正则化”（ROR）概念，提出跨源风格指纹图（CSFG）框架，将位置、序列、语义及过渡偏差编码为可学习的GNN边特征，利用每边符号偏差δ_ij作为假阳性校准器。

Result: 在四个基准测试中验证ROR显著性（p < 0.001）；二元检测准确率达97.14%，优于最强图基线11.14个百分点，假阳性率仅1.57%，对未见过的LLM具有鲁棒泛化性（但在过渡方差低于人类基线的生成器上性能下降）。

Conclusion: 揭示LLM在段落边界和模板化过渡中产生的句子间转换方差异常是检测关键；CSFG通过操作化ROR实现了高效、低误报的通用检测，证明了关系建模比单纯架构创新更具价值。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated text (AIGT) remains challenging because existing approaches rely on token-level statistical signals or independent stylometric features, causing them to overfit to specific generators and fail under distribution shift. We identify a structural signal at the sentence-pair level: LLMs produce inter-sentence transition variance that deviates from human writing through inflated variance driven by recurring similarity bursts at paragraph boundaries and templated transitions. We formalize this as Relational Over-Regularization (ROR) and validate it across four benchmarks (p < 0.001). The central contribution is this relational problem formulation, not a novel GNN architecture; CSFG is one concrete instantiation for operationalizing ROR. To exploit this signal, we propose the Cross-Source Stylometric Fingerprint Graph (CSFG), a graph-based framework that encodes positional, sequential, semantic, and transition deviation signals as learnable GNN edge features. The per-edge signed deviation δ_ij operationalizes ROR without hand-crafted thresholds and acts as a false-positive calibrator. CSFG achieves 97.14% accuracy under binary detection, outperforming the strongest graph-based baseline by 11.14 pp, with a false-positive rate of 1.57% and robust generalization to unseen LLMs in the inflated-variance regime; detection degrades for generators whose transition variance falls at or below the human baseline.

</details>


### [285] [Five Primitives for Governing Autonomous AI Agents at Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26696)
*Jiten Oswal,John Cadeddu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自主AI智能体在部署中因临时性、模型驱动行为及API发现特性导致的控制失效，提出将治理视为运行时问题，并构建了包含发现、身份、治理、证明和供应链的五项原语架构。该架构通过策略中介、租户级授权和哈希签名账本实现执行与验证，目前五项原语中有四项已在私有试点中运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署的自主AI智能体继承了为人类用户和长期服务设计的控制模型，但在三个方面存在适配失败：智能体主体是临时的（出现和消失速度快于配置）、其行为由模型选择而非编程（未来可能尝试的行为不可预知）、以及其群体是通过API调用发现的而非配置的。因此，现有的构建时或模型对齐方法无法有效治理这些智能体。

Method: 作者论证治理是一个运行时问题，并从“行动生效前”和“行动发生后”两个阶段的问题中推导出五个核心原语：发现（Discovery）、身份（Identity）、治理（Governance）、证明（Attestation）和供应链（Supply Chain）。实施方面，对智能体的行动在生效前进行策略中介，基于每个租户的动作词汇表进行授权，并将行动记录在第三方可通过供应商离线验证的哈希链接签名账本中。

Result: 分析了架构的成本：执行点位于请求的关键路径上；身份识别需要为每个工作负载配备sidecar组件；关闭式（fail-closed）中介机制会将可用性故障转化为拒绝服务。实现状态显示，四个原语已构建并在私有试点中运行，第五个原语作为独立工具构建但尚未集成到请求路径中。

Conclusion: 当前的五部分分解并非一个通用的分类学，而是对其代码库实现的描述。这表明自主AI智能体的治理必须解决运行时特有的结构性和操作性挑战，且目前的实现仍处于早期试点阶段，需在性能开销和安全性之间权衡。

Abstract: Enterprise deployments of autonomous AI agents inherit a control model built for human users and long-lived services, and the fit fails in three specific ways: agent principals are ephemeral, appearing and vanishing faster than provisioning; their actions are selected by a model rather than programmed, so the set of things they may attempt is not known in advance; and the population is discovered rather than provisioned, because anyone who can call an API can create one.
